- **compositor-letras-canciones-en**: Interviews the user step by step and writes the complete lyrics of an original song, tailored to what they share. It activates when the user asks for help writing song lyrics and does not know how to structure them. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/en-agents-1/agents/compositor-letras-canciones-en.md))
- **compositor-no-save-restore**: The fundamental problem: save_buffer is a **point-in-time snapshot** of hw_fb, but the VT backing buffer is the **source of truth** for what should be under the cursor. Any approach that caches hw_fb pixels will go stale. ([chris17453/oxide_os](https://github.com/chris17453/oxide_os/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/compositor-no-save-restore.md) · ★3)
